<div id="product-description" class="container container--main">

    <div class="productDescription container">

        <div class="productDescription__title">
            <h2>Product Details</h2>
        </div>

        <div class="productDescription-content">
            <div class="productDescription-content__description
                ">
                <div class="richtext">
                    <p>Formulated with three essential ceramides, <a href="https://www.cerave.com/ingredients-in-cerave/hyaluronic-acid">hyaluronic acid</a>, <a href="https://www.cerave.com/skincare/dimethicone">dimethicone</a>, and <a href="https://www.cerave.com/skincare/petrolatum">petrolatum</a>, this deeply hydrating moisturizing cream helps restore and maintain the skin&rsquo;s natural protective barrier&mdash;which helps lock in moisture and keep harmful irritants out. It also contains our unique MVE Delivery Technology that continually releases these ingredients for long-lasting hydration, leading to more comfortable skin that looks and feels smoother, softer, and healthier. Unlike many other dry skin moisturizers&mdash;even other moisturizers with ceramides&mdash;this moisturizing cream has a rich texture, yet it&rsquo;s non-greasy, absorbs in a flash, and won&rsquo;t leave behind any gunky residue.</p>
<ul>
<li>This face and body moisturizing cream has a rich texture, yet absorbs quickly, without a greasy feel.</li>
<li>Three essential ceramides join forces with hyaluronic acid, dimethicone, and petrolatum to help infuse dry skin with moisture and lock hydration in.</li>
<li>MVE Delivery Technology ensures a continuous release of long-lasting hydration.</li>
<li>With continued use, this moisturizer with ceramides helps restore and maintain dry skin&rsquo;s damaged protective barrier.</li>
<li>Developed with <a href="https://www.cerave.com/about-cerave/developed-with-dermatologists">dermatologists</a>, this moisturizing cream is gentle enough for the whole family (ages 3 years and up).</li>
</ul>
                </div>
            </div>

        </div>

            </li>

                               event-topic="Which skin types should use CeraVe Moisturizing Cream?">
                        <template #heading>
<h2>Which skin types should use CeraVe Moisturizing Cream?</h2>                        </template>
                        <p>Most <a href="https://www.cerave.com/skin-smarts/skincare-tips-advice/what-skin-type-do-i-have">skin types</a> can use CeraVe Moisturizing Cream due to its gentle formula, which is fragrance-free, allergy-tested, and non-comedogenic (meaning it won’t clog pores). It’s also been developed with <a href="https://www.cerave.com/find-a-dermatologist">dermatologists</a> and is accepted by the National Eczema Association. That said, this moisturizer with ceramides can be especially beneficial for those with <a href="https://www.cerave.com/skin-smarts/skin-concerns/dry-skin">normal to dry skin</a>. </p>

                    </accordion>
                </li>
                <li>
                    <accordion title="Can I use this barrier-restoring moisturizing cream on my face and body?"
                               expand-label="Expand"
                               collapse-label="Collapse"
                               event-category="faq"
                               event-label="Can I use this barrier-restoring moisturizing cream on my face and body?"
                               event-name="faq"
                               event-action="select::topic"
                               event-topic="Can I use this barrier-restoring moisturizing cream on my face and body?">
                        <template #heading>
<h2>Can I use this barrier-restoring moisturizing cream on my face and body?</h2>                        </template>
                        <p>
Yes. CeraVe Moisturizing Cream can be applied anywhere to the skin on your face and body to help restore the appearance of hydrated, healthy-looking skin (as well as the comfort that goes along with it). 
</p>
                    </accordion>
                </li>
                <li>
                    <accordion title="What’s the difference between CeraVe Moisturizing Cream and CeraVe Daily Moisturizing Cream?"
                               expand-label="Expand"
                               collapse-label="Collapse"
                               event-category="faq"
                               event-label="What’s the difference between CeraVe Moisturizing Cream and CeraVe Daily Moisturizing Cream?"
                               event-name="faq"
                               event-action="select::topic"
                               event-topic="What’s the difference between CeraVe Moisturizing Cream and CeraVe Daily Moisturizing Cream?">
                        <template #heading>
<h2>What’s the difference between CeraVe Moisturizing Cream and CeraVe Daily Moisturizing Cream?</h2>                        </template>
                        <p>The main difference between these two moisturizers with ceramides is their texture. CeraVe
Moisturizing Cream features a rich yet non-greasy cream formula ideal for those with normal
to dry skin on their face or body. It contains <a href="https://www.cerave.com/skincare/petrolatum">petrolatum</a>, an occlusive ingredient that helps seal in moisture by creating a physical barrier on the skin’s surface. <a href="https://www.cerave.com/skincare/moisturizers/daily-moisturizing-lotion">CeraVe Daily
Moisturizing Lotion</a>, on the other hand, is designed with a lightweight, oil-free formula that
may be better suited for <a href="https://www.cerave.com/skincare/normal-skin">normal</a> to <a href="https://www.cerave.com/skincare/oily-skin">oily</a> skin on your face or body. It also boasts beneficial
ingredients such as glycerin and dimethicone.</p>
                    </accordion>
                </li>
                <li>
                    <accordion title="Why is a barrier-restoring moisturizing cream important in my skincare routine?"
                               expand-label="Expand"
                               collapse-label="Collapse"
                               event-category="faq"
                               event-label="Why is a barrier-restoring moisturizing cream important in my skincare routine?"
                               event-name="faq"
                               event-action="select::topic"
                               event-topic="Why is a barrier-restoring moisturizing cream important in my skincare routine?">
                        <template #heading>
<h2>Why is a barrier-restoring moisturizing cream important in my skincare routine?</h2>                        </template>
                        <p>Your skin&rsquo;s natural moisture barrier (formally known as the stratum corneum) is essential for healthy-looking skin. When it&rsquo;s functioning properly, it plays a vital role in helping to keep moisture in and irritants out. However, if it becomes disrupted, it can cause a whole slew of skin issues (including dry, <a href="https://www.cerave.com/skin-smarts/skin-concerns/itchy-skin/evaluating-your-itchy-skin-is-it-an-allergic-reaction-of-something-more-serious">itchy skin</a>) and even cause chronic skin conditions, such as <a href="https://www.cerave.com/skincare/eczema">eczema</a>, to flare up. That&rsquo;s why incorporating a skin barrier-restoring moisturizer with ceramides, like CeraVe Moisturizing Cream, into your daily <a href="https://www.cerave.com/skin-smarts/skincare-routines/a-body-care-routine-for-dry-skin">skincare regimen</a> can be a smart step to help both repair and maintain your skin&rsquo;s delicate protective barrier.</p>
                    </accordion>
                </li>
                <li>
                    <accordion title="What makes this moisturizing cream different from other dry skin moisturizers?"
                               expand-label="Expand"
                               collapse-label="Collapse"
                               event-category="faq"
                               event-label="What makes this moisturizing cream different from other dry skin moisturizers?"
                               event-name="faq"
                               event-action="select::topic"
                               event-topic="What makes this moisturizing cream different from other dry skin moisturizers?">
                        <template #heading>
<h2>What makes this moisturizing cream different from other dry skin moisturizers?</h2>                        </template>
                        <p>Like many dry skin moisturizers, CeraVe Moisturizing Cream helps hydrate and soothe skin, while also helping to reduce uncomfortable dryness. However, what makes all of CeraVe formulas special is their unique blend of three essential ceramides. Our moisturizer with ceramides was developed with dermatologists and clinically tested to help ensure it effectively locks in moisture and helps restore and maintain your skin&rsquo;s protective barrier. Our moisturizing cream also features MVE Delivery Technology to help release a steady stream of moisturizing ingredients for long-lasting hydration.</p>
                    </accordion>
                </li>
                <li>
                    <accordion title="How often should I apply CeraVe Moisturizing Cream?"
                               expand-label="Expand"
                               collapse-label="Collapse"
                               event-category="faq"
                               event-label="How often should I apply CeraVe Moisturizing Cream?"
                               event-name="faq"
                               event-action="select::topic"
                               event-topic="How often should I apply CeraVe Moisturizing Cream?">
                        <template #heading>
<h2>How often should I apply CeraVe Moisturizing Cream?</h2>                        </template>
                        <p>We’ll leave that up to you! You can apply our gentle, fast-absorbing, non-greasy Moisturizing Cream as little as twice daily (morning and night) to your face and body, or as needed whenever your skin is feeling dry. To help gain more moisturizing savviness, explore some of our top tips in our article <a href="https://www.cerave.com/skin-smarts/skincare-tips-advice/best-moisturizer-dermatologist-recommended">Moisturizing 101</a>.</p>
                    </accordion>
                </li>
                <li>
                    <accordion title="Do I need a moisturizing cream if I have oily skin?"
                               expand-label="Expand"
                               collapse-label="Collapse"
                               event-category="faq"
                               event-label="Do I need a moisturizing cream if I have oily skin?"
                               event-name="faq"
                               event-action="select::topic"
                               event-topic="Do I need a moisturizing cream if I have oily skin?">
                        <template #heading>
                                <h2>Do I need a moisturizing cream if I have oily skin?</h2>
                        </template>
                        <p><a href="https://www.cerave.com/skincare/oily-skin">Oily skin</a> is caused by an overproduction of sebum (oil), which is what can give it a shiny, greasy-looking appearance. But that doesn&rsquo;t mean that oily skin doesn&rsquo;t need moisturizer too. In fact, oily skin requires just as much moisture as other skin types. Plus, not moisturizing oily skin can actually be counterproductive. When skin isn&rsquo;t properly hydrated, it may overcompensate and produce even more oil. So, it&rsquo;s usually best to be strategic about which type of moisturizing cream you choose.</p>
<p>For oily or <a href="https://www.cerave.com/skin-smarts/skin-concerns/acne">acne-prone</a> skin, we recommend choosing a lightweight moisturizer with ceramides that&rsquo;s &ldquo;non-comedogenic,&rdquo; which means it won&rsquo;t clog your pores. Try <a href="https://www.cerave.com/skincare/moisturizers/daily-moisturizing-lotion">CeraVe Daily Moisturizing Lotion</a>, a lightweight, oil-free moisturizer with three essential ceramides. It also helps lock in your skin's moisture and restore your protective barrier.</p>
                    </accordion>
                </li>
                <li>
                    <accordion title="Which CeraVe moisturizing cream should I use for my baby’s skin?"
                               expand-label="Expand"
                               collapse-label="Collapse"
                               event-category="faq"
                               event-label="Which CeraVe moisturizing cream should I use for my baby’s skin?"
                               event-name="faq"
                               event-action="select::topic"
                               event-topic="Which CeraVe moisturizing cream should I use for my baby’s skin?">
                        <template #heading>
                                <h2>Which CeraVe moisturizing cream should I use for my baby’s skin?</h2>
                        </template>
                        <p>To help care for and protect your <a href="https://www.cerave.com/skin-smarts/skincare-for-baby/3-reasons-why-your-babys-skin-may-be-dry-and-how-to-care-for-it">baby&rsquo;s delicate skin</a>, we recommend <a href="https://www.cerave.com/skincare/baby/baby-moisturizing-cream">CeraVe Baby Moisturizing Cream</a>. This gentle, lightweight <a href="https://www.cerave.com/skincare/baby">baby lotion</a> was developed with pediatric dermatologists to deliver three essential ceramides and help maintain your infant's skin barrier as it develops. Formulated with vitamin E and hyaluronic acid, our Baby Moisturizing Cream is non-greasy and free of parabens, dyes, and fragrance. To learn more about how to care for your baby&rsquo;s skin, browse our <a href="https://www.cerave.com/skin-smarts/skincare-for-baby">tips and advice on caring for baby's delicate skin</a>. And keep in mind that if your little one develops dry skin as they grow into toddlerhood, CeraVe Moisturizing Cream can be used on kids ages 3 and up!</p>
                    </accordion>
                </li>
        </ul>
